Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The ceramics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 969 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 213 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the ceramics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 969 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 213. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)
